([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-accuracy-comparison))
- **cross-dataset-algorithm-comparison**: Use when a new alignment or feature-correspondence method must be validated against established software on ≥2 benchmark datasets with ground-truth manual annotations, and per-dataset accuracy deltas are the target output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-algorithm-comparison))
- **cross-dataset-assembly-metric-aggregation**: Use when comparing de novo transcriptome assembler quality across datasets whose gene-detection depth differs, and raw counts of 95%-assembled genes are not directly comparable without Kallisto-based normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cross-dataset-assembly-metric-aggregation))
- **cross-dataset-barcode-precision-validation**: Use when you need to quantify false cell barcode or UMI assignment rates for Nanopore reads, or to validate Nanopore-assigned cellBC/UMI pairs against an independent Illumina short-read dataset from the same sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cross-dataset-barcode-precision-validation))
- **cross-dataset-batch-effect-evaluation**: Use when comparing reference-based cell-type mapping methods across two single-cell datasets with batch effect and strong asymmetry in cell number and library depth (e.g., whole-cell scRNA-seq reference to ultra-sparse snRNA-seq nuclei target). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-batch-effect-evaluation))
- **cross-dataset-benchmark-comparison**: Use when a spectral/annotation method's accuracy claim must be validated across two or more independent test sets (e.g. NPLIB1 vs MassSpecGym) rather than a single benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-benchmark-comparison))
- **cross-dataset-benchmark-comparison**: Use when comparing competing feature sets or representations for cell clustering across several scRNA-seq datasets, requiring replicate-level metrics (AMI) against known subpopulation labels to show consistency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-benchmark-comparison))
- **cross-dataset-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when validating an automatic cluster annotation method on several independent published scRNA-seq datasets with known manual annotations, to report per-dataset consistency rates and an overall average. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-benchmark-evaluation--2ec19ce106481f14))
- **cross-dataset-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a classifier's robustness must be benchmarked across multiple independently sequenced scRNA-seq datasets (e.g., 6 cross-platform human pancreas training-test pairs), comparing 2+ annotation methods by mean classification accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-benchmark-evaluation--a47b7d7438264cc8))
- **cross-dataset-benchmark-reproduction**: Use when a target paper reports a metrics table across multiple datasets or evaluation conditions and the goal is to independently re-run the proposed method plus baselines to verify the reported ordering and values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/cross-dataset-benchmark-reproduction))
- **cross-dataset-bgc-cross-referencing**: Use when mass spectrometry identifications from multiple GNPS microbial datasets can be paired with genome mining data from the same strains and you need to validate identifications or discover novel BGC-to-molecule links. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-bgc-cross-referencing))
- **cross-dataset-biomarker-reproducibility-assessment**: Use when a biomarker-discovery pipeline (e.g. CAMAMED with cumulative sum-scaling and metagenomeSeq) has produced case-control marker taxa/genes from one metagenome dataset and an independent dataset from the same study context is available to test whether the markers reproduce. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/cross-dataset-biomarker-reproducibility-assessment))
- **cross-dataset-cell-annotation-evaluation**: Use when you have 2+ annotated scRNA-seq reference datasets with batch effects and a held-out unlabeled target dataset, and you need cross-dataset cell-type annotation plus an evaluation against known labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-cell-annotation-evaluation))
- **cross-dataset-cell-embedding-evaluation**: Use when scRNA-seq datasets with overlapping cell types must be integrated and you need to quantify dataset mixing vs. cell-type resolution for competing correction methods (e.g., ComBat, MNN, CCA, Harmony, scVI, DIRECTi). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-cell-embedding-evaluation))
- **cross-dataset-cell-label-integration**: Use when per-dataset cell subtype fine labels have been produced (fine-labels.RDS per dataset) and you need to reconcile or compare subtype label definitions across published references by constructing a cross-dataset Jaccard similarity matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-cell-label-integration))
- **cross-dataset-cell-population-matching**: Use when two or more annotated scRNA-seq datasets (batch-corrected/aligned, e.g. with scVI or Seurat) must have their cell population labels matched and a hierarchical classification tree learned, especially when name-based matching fails (e.g. different labs, resolutions, or nomenclatures). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-cell-population-matching))
- **cross-dataset-cell-type-mapping**: Use when a labeled reference scRNA-seq dataset and an unlabeled target dataset (e.g., Drop-seq reference to Smart-seq2 target, or Smart-Seq2 to CEL-Seq pancreas) must be mapped across technologies or batch conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-cell-type-mapping))
- **cross-dataset-cell-type-prediction**: Use when a labeled training scRNA-seq dataset (h5ad) must annotate cells from a different dataset — same species or cross-species — where the test set contains cell types with no annotated cells in the training data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-cell-type-prediction))
- **cross-dataset-cell-type-replicability-assessment**: Use when you need to measure whether cell types (e.g., neurons vs non-neurons, excitatory vs inhibitory, interneuron subtypes) identified in one scRNA-seq study replicate in independent studies, or to find reciprocal top-hit cell-type pairs across datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-cell-type-replicability-assessment))
- **cross-dataset-cell-type-transfer**: Use when a pre-annotated reference scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., .h5ad from GEO) must be used to label cells in a different test dataset whose taxonomy may contain unseen cell types, and errors from noise/batch effects need suppression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-cell-type-transfer))
- **cross-dataset-classifier-comparison**: Use when a new cell-annotation or classification method must be compared against established algorithms on the same ground-truth-labeled scRNA-seq test sets with ranked, per-dataset metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-classifier-comparison))
- **cross-dataset-clustering-benchmark-aggregation**: Use when benchmarking a clustering model across multiple scRNA-seq datasets and random seeds (e.g. 20 datasets, seeds 1111–10000) and you must assemble per-dataset mean/median ARI comparisons into one table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-clustering-benchmark-aggregation))
- **cross-dataset-clustering-comparison**: Use when you need to compare a scRNA-seq cell clustering method's accuracy (ARI) against published tools across many datasets with known cell type labels, e.g. benchmarking scQA on GEO/ArrayExpress data such as GSE57249 or E-MTAB-5061. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-clustering-comparison))
- **cross-dataset-clustering-ordering-comparison**: Use when you have clustered spectra from ≥2 MS/MS clustering tools on ≥2 metabolomics datasets and need MS-RT purity/N10 scores to test whether tool rankings (e.g. Falcon vs MaRaCluster) transfer across instruments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-clustering-ordering-comparison))
- **cross-dataset-coexpression-reproducibility-assessment**: Use when multiple methods infer cell-type-specific co-expression from scRNA-seq data and you must test which method's significant gene pairs replicate in an independent dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-coexpression-reproducibility-assessment))
- **cross-dataset-component-comparability-handling**: Use when decomposing individual tumor scRNA-seq expression matrices with ICA (n_components=100) and you need gene loading scales and orientations comparable across components and datasets before downstream clustering or pan-cancer integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-component-comparability-handling))
- **cross-dataset-consistency-analysis**: Use when differential abundance results exist for the same phenotype across multiple independent datasets (e.g., five diarrhea or five obesity studies) and you need to quantify which DA tools call the same genera significant more often than expected by chance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/cross-dataset-consistency-analysis))
- **cross-dataset-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when the goal is to identify genes consistently dysregulated across several public transcriptomics cohorts (e.g. severe vs mild COVID-19 peripheral blood RNA-seq) instead of relying on a single-dataset DE result. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-differential-expression-analysis))
- **cross-dataset-drug-response-scale-transformation**: Use when drug response data come from datasets with incompatible units (e.g., GDSC IC50 vs PRISM Repurposing 19Q4 AUC) and must be placed on a common sensitivity scale before model training or cross-dataset correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-drug-response-scale-transformation))
- **cross-dataset-empathy-benchmark-combination**: Use when the goal is to reproduce or exceed the empathy prediction scores (PCC/CCC/RMSE) on the NewsEmp24 test set by training on the combined NewsEmp24+23+22 data with noise-mitigated labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/cross-dataset-empathy-benchmark-combination))
- **cross-dataset-entry-filtering**: Use when you have received MSBERT-preprocessed spectral data from GNPS, MoNA, or MTBLS1572 and need to ensure data integrity before training a spectral embedding model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-entry-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **cross-dataset-expression-comparison**: Use when validating that simulated scRNA-seq data reproduce a protocol-specific expression property (e.g., gene length bias in non-UMI data) by direct comparison against a matched experimental dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-expression-comparison))
- **cross-dataset-expression-correlation-mapping**: Use when unsupervised BUSseq clusters need biological labels beyond FACS labels — e.g., verifying an intermediate CMP-MEP cluster or mapping a CLP/LMPP-like cluster to Haemopedia progenitor populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-expression-correlation-mapping))
- **cross-dataset-feature-correspondence-mapping**: Use when you have two or more nontargeted LCMS feature tables from the same analytical method (same column, ionization mode, and acquisition parameters) and need to identify which features in one dataset correspond to features in another. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-feature-correspondence-mapping) · 2 copies)
- **cross-dataset-feature-match-audit**: Use when two LC–MS metabolomics feature tables have already been matched (e.g., 4953 unique matches via M2S) and you must verify correctness using the subset of features annotated at MSI confidence level 2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-feature-match-audit))
- **cross-dataset-feature-matching**: Use when you have two or more feature tables in HDF5 format with detected features characterized by m/z, drift time, retention time, and intensity, and you need to match corresponding features across samples to account for systematic shifts caused by instrument variation or tuning differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-feature-matching) · 3 copies)
- **cross-dataset-feature-matching**: Use when two peak-picked, conventionally aligned untargeted LC-MS feature tables acquired under non-identical conditions (different gradients, instruments, or laboratories) must have their overlapping features identified and concatenated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-feature-matching--2e60be99828f37d3))
- **cross-dataset-feature-matching**: Use when integrating two untargeted LC-MS metabolomics peak tables (e.g. MESA reference vs Rotterdam target) that share RT, m/z, and FI dimensions but were acquired in separate runs, and you need unique feature correspondence without MS/MS or annotation-level matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-feature-matching--df4b4cd467b46b3a))
- **cross-dataset-feature-matching-evaluation**: Use when you have completed feature matching between two LC-MS metabolomics datasets and need to quantify match correctness — e.g. via annotated feature concordance or a simulated dataset with known embedded correspondences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-feature-matching-evaluation))
- **cross-dataset-gene-classification-benchmarking**: Use when you need to classify which of the top-2000 Seurat FindVariableFeatures() HVGs participate in cell-cell communication, and compare the classified gene counts across independent spatial transcriptomics datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-gene-classification-benchmarking))
- **cross-dataset-gene-clustering**: Use when a published gene signature (e.g. Bailey ADEX) may be confounded by tissue-nonspecific or non-tumor genes, and you have ≥2 independently parsed bulk RNAseq datasets to test cross-dataset reproducibility of gene groupings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-gene-clustering))
- **cross-dataset-gene-set-intersection**: Use when building robust cancer-cell classification features by intersecting malignant-vs-non-malignant DEGs across multiple training scRNA-seq datasets (e.g., RCC, HNSCC, melanoma, LUAD, BC) rather than relying on a single dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-gene-set-intersection))
- **cross-dataset-generalization-assessment**: Use when you have a pre-trained MS/MS spectral embedding model evaluated on one or more source datasets (GNPS, MoNA, MTBLS1572) and need to verify that it performs well on independent, high-quality curated spectral libraries to claim robustness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-generalization-assessment) · 2 copies)
- **cross-dataset-generalization-evaluation**: Use when you need to test whether a CCS prediction model's reported accuracy transfers beyond its training dataset's chemical space, using METLIN-CCS (~27k structures) and CCSBase v1.3 (16,989 values) as train/test pairs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-generalization-evaluation))
- **cross-dataset-generalization-metrics-aggregation**: Use when a trained GNN CCS predictor must be evaluated on a structurally different database than its training set (e.g. CCSBase→METLIN-CCS or METLIN-CCS→CCSBase) and per-model R2/MAE/MRE must be compiled into a cross-database comparison table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-generalization-metrics-aggregation))
- **cross-dataset-hic-benchmark-comparison**: Use when benchmarking per-cell informative chromatin interaction counts across single-cell Hi-C modalities, or when harmonizing published valid-pair datasets (e.g. GSM2254217, GSE174226) under identical distance and deduplication filters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/cross-dataset-hic-benchmark-comparison))
- **cross-dataset-intensity-normalization**: Use when combining probe-level microarray intensity data from multiple GEO series (e.g. 3T3-L1 differentiation studies) where per-study platforms and processing differ and cross-study comparability is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-intensity-normalization))
- **cross-dataset-joins**: Patterns for combining data from multiple Montréal datasets. Includes borough-based joins, proximity joins, temporal joins, and strategies for working around CKAN's single-table SQL limitation. / Stratégies pour combiner des données de plusieurs jeux de données : jointures par arrondissement, par proximité, par période, et contournement de la limitation SQL mono-table de CKAN. ([alistaircroll/montreal-open-data](https://github.com/alistaircroll/montreal-open-data/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/cross-dataset-joins) · ★1)
- **cross-dataset-map-aggregation**: Use when comparing two annotation scoring methods across a corpus of imaging mass spectrometry datasets and you need a single ranking-quality metric (MAP) per method per sample type, plus counts of datasets with statistically significant AP differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-map-aggregation))
- **cross-dataset-mass-signature-pooling**: Use when multiple replicate DESI-MSI datasets of the same tissue type (e.g., root sections) have each been processed along a linear developmental gradient and you need a pooled, de-replicated mass signature list for cross-sample clustering and variety comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-mass-signature-pooling))
- **cross-dataset-masst-search**: Use when an annotation made in one full-scan dataset (e.g. a pseudo MS/MS spectrum from NIST feces MSV000095787) must be validated or propagated by finding the top cosine match in a pool of pseudo MS/MS spectra from another dataset (e.g. IBD, PR000639). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-masst-search))
- **cross-dataset-match-count-reporting**: Use when an Eclipse MSAligner one-by-all alignment has been run (e.g., a reference dataset like rat plasma DS5 against comparison datasets DS6–DS9) and you must report how many of the reference features matched in all datasets, partially matched, or had no match. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-match-count-reporting))
- **cross-dataset-metadata-tabulation**: Use when a collection of parsed expression datasets must be inventoried into a single annotation table with consistent columns (measurement type, sample description, n samples, n features, Used flag), e.g. to audit a compendium or reproduce a published dataset table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-metadata-tabulation))
- **cross-dataset-metagene-network-construction**: Use when two ICA decompositions of different cancer transcriptomic datasets must be compared component-wise to assess reproducibility, and no one-to-one component ordering exists between them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-metagene-network-construction))
- **cross-dataset-method-comparison**: Use when a choice among feature-selection (top-value) or partitioning methods for consensus clustering must be justified by large-scale empirical comparison across many datasets rather than a single example. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-method-comparison))
- **cross-dataset-model-benchmarking**: Use when you must quantify how much a model component (e.g. predicted fingerprint input) contributes by re-running the identical generation/re-ranking pipeline with that component removed on held-out spectra from multiple reference datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-model-benchmarking))
- **cross-dataset-model-evaluation**: Use when a vision-language meme-detection model has been trained (or ablated) and must be benchmarked on more than one dataset with dataset-specific metrics such as ACC/AUROC for Hateful Memes and ACC/F1 for MultiOFF. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/cross-dataset-model-evaluation))
- **cross-dataset-model-generalization-assessment**: Use when you have trained a neural network or regression model on one paired microbiome-metabolome dataset and wish to test whether it can predict metabolite abundances in an independent, externally-sourced dataset collected from different patient cohorts or study populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-model-generalization-assessment) · 2 copies)
- **cross-dataset-model-generalization-testing**: Use when a trained model artifact (e.g., scripted_m21.pth) must be evaluated on held-out BAMs from organisms/chemistry differing from its training set, to quantify generalization via AUC/F1 before deployment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cross-dataset-model-generalization-testing))
- **cross-dataset-model-transfer**: Use when a trained scRNA-seq cell-type classifier must be transferred to a second dataset with different cells but overlapping cell-type labels, and prediction accuracy/F1 must be quantified against the target dataset's known labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-model-transfer))
- **cross-dataset-model-transfer-assessment**: Use when a pre-trained model (e.g. aptardi's HBR model.hdf5) must be tested for generalizability across RNA-Seq datasets differing in tissue, organism, library preparation, or sequencing depth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-model-transfer-assessment))
- **cross-dataset-model-transfer-evaluation**: Use when you have candidate-feature tables (e.g. dsRID_whole.tsv) from multiple ENCODE long-read RNA-seq datasets and need to decide whether a self-trained random forest or the PacBio AD-trained transferred model performs better under 20-fold cross-validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-model-transfer-evaluation))
- **cross-dataset-model-transfer-validation**: Use when ML models trained on one cell-line panel (e.g. CCLE) have produced top features, and you need to confirm those features generalize by measuring their importance in independent datasets such as NCI60 or single-cell data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-model-transfer-validation))
- **cross-dataset-molecule-deduplication**: Use when building a screening library from multiple structure databases (SDF/gzipped SDF) and non-organic, ionic, or duplicate molecules must be removed before spectrum prediction or other downstream chemistry. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-molecule-deduplication))
- **cross-dataset-molecule-matching**: Use when predicted RTs from one chromatographic method must be made comparable to RTs measured under independent experimental reverse-phase methods, and per-method projection errors (e.g. median absolute error in seconds) must be quantified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-dataset-molecule-matching))
- **cross-dataset-ms-alignment-benchmarking**: Use when you must reproduce or extend a head-to-head comparison of LC-MS alignment tools (e.g. metabCombiner vs Eclipse) on multiple same-method datasets and report overlap and spurious-match counts. - **cross-dataset-network-consistency-analysis**: Use when you have several bulk transcriptomic cohorts with matched survival data (e.g., metastatic melanoma datasets like TCGA SKCM06) and need to test whether inferred cell-cell interaction networks are reproducible across cohorts and across different cell-type marker annotations. - **cross-dataset-node-importance-comparison**: Use when you have scBONITA rule-inference node importance scores for KEGG pathway networks from one or more scRNA-seq datasets and need to check whether importance scores carry context-dependent signaling information beyond topology alone. - **cross-dataset-overlap-analysis**: Use when a list of candidate microbial MS/MS spectra (.mgf from MZmine/GNPS networking) must be cross-validated against an independent public dataset with treatment-vs-control metadata to test biological exclusivity. - **cross-dataset-peak-intensity-correlation**: Use when reanalyzing publicly deposited MRM mzML raw data to verify that an automatic peak-integration pipeline reproduces previously reported, manually reviewed peak intensities on a per-metabolite basis. - **cross-dataset-peak-overlap-analysis**: Use when multiple peak files from different epigenomic assays (e.g. CUT&Tag, CUT&Run, ChIP-seq BED/narrowPeak) must be benchmarked against a reference for QC, overlap statistics, and functional annotation. - **cross-dataset-peak-recovery-assessment**: Use when replicating or validating an MSI peak-identification pipeline against a published peak list (e.g., the 63 peaks of Zhang et al. 2023) using multiple replicate DESI-MSI datasets. - **cross-dataset-performance-delta-tabulation**: Use when a paper reports a multi-dataset ablation grid (e.g., CoT chain counts n and per-perspective chains m) and you need to compute ablated-minus-full metric deltas (ACC, AUROC, F1) per dataset and visualize them. - **cross-dataset-promoter-comparison**: Use when two or more transcription start site (TSS) datasets for the same organism must be benchmarked against each other, and a motif-positioning-based quality comparison of nascent vs. annotated TSSs is desired. - **cross-dataset-read-depth-normalization**: Use when circRNA BSJ or isoform counts must be compared across independent datasets (e.g., isoCirc nanopore libraries vs. short-read RNA-seq) generated at different sequencing depths over multiple tissues. - **cross-dataset-reference-enrichment-analysis**: Use when cell type-specific genomic features (loops, peaks, enhancers) from one dataset must be validated against independent cell type-matched epigenomic or transcriptomic reference datasets. - **cross-dataset-reference-evaluation**: Use when you need to evaluate or tune a reference-based cell-type identifier (e.g. MarkerCount_Ref) without holding out a dedicated training set, by rotating multiple annotated scRNA-seq datasets through test/reference roles. - **cross-dataset-score-distribution-comparison**: Use when when you have applied multiple scoring functions (e.g., strain correlation and IOKR) to rank genomic-metabolomic (GCF-MF or BGC-spectrum) links and need to verify that: (1) standardisation produces zero mean and unit variance across all links; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cross-dataset-score-distribution-comparison) · 2 copies)
- **cross-dataset-shared-gene-merging**: Use when two or more scRNA-seq AnnData objects with partially non-overlapping (or homologous but differently ordered) gene lists must be merged into a single cells-by-genes matrix before integration, transfer, or runtime benchmarking. - **cross-dataset-spectral-comparison**: Use when you hold MGF peak lists (with precursor m/z) from two LC-MS/MS datasets and want to compare spectral similarity versus precursor m/z difference pre-database-search, e.g. human vs. chimpanzee or human vs. E. coli serum.
